```tsx
// src/components/LiqPassProductPage.tsx
import React, { useState } from "react";

type Locale = "zh-CN" | "en-US";
type Localized = { zh: string; en?: string } | string;

function makeLocalizedMessage(zh: string, en?: string): Localized {
  return { zh, en: en ?? zh };
}
function getLocalized(msg: Localized, locale: Locale) {
  if (typeof msg === "string") return msg;
  return locale === "en-US" ? msg.en ?? msg.zh : msg.zh;
}

const UX_PRODUCTS = [
  {
    id: "OKX-DAY-100",
    name: makeLocalizedMessage("当日爆仓保 · 定额赔付 100 USDT", "Same-day liquidation cover · Flat payout 100 USDT"),
    premium: makeLocalizedMessage("10 USDC", "10 USDC"),
    leverageCap: makeLocalizedMessage("≤ 20x", "≤ 20x"),
    payoutCap: makeLocalizedMessage("100 USDT", "100 USDT"),
    waitPeriod: makeLocalizedMessage("30 分钟等待期", "30-minute waiting period"),
  },
];

export default function LiqPassProductPage({
  locale = "zh-CN",
  onSelect,
  initialId,
}: {
  locale?: Locale;
  onSelect?: (id: string) => void;
  initialId?: string;
}) {
  const [selectedProductId, setSelectedProductId] = useState<string>(
    initialId ?? UX_PRODUCTS[0]?.id ?? ""
  );
  const L = (m: Localized) => getLocalized(m, locale);

  const handleSelect = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedProductId(id);
    onSelect?.(id);
  };

  return (
    <section className="space-y-4">
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold">
        {L({ zh: "合约保险产品列表", en: "Insurance product catalog" })}
      </h2>

      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
        {UX_PRODUCTS.map((product) => {
          const active = product.id === selectedProductId;
          return (
            <button
              type="button"
              key={product.id}
              onClick={() => handleSelect(product.id)}
              className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 text-left transition ${
                active
                  ? "border-indigo-400 bg-indigo-400/10 shadow-lg shadow-indigo-500/20"
                  : "border-white/10 bg-white/5 hover:border-indigo-400/60"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs uppercase tracking-widest">
                <span className="text-indigo-200/80">{product.id}</span>
                {active && (
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-indigo-500/30 px-2 py-0.5 text-indigo-100">
                    {L({ zh: "当前", en: "Selected" })}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>

              <h3 className="mt-3 text-lg font-semibold text-white">
                {L(product.name)}
              </h3>

              <ul className="mt-4 space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-200/80">
                <li>
                  {L({ zh: "保费：", en: "Premium: " })}
                  {L(product.premium)}
                </li>
                <li>
                  {L({ zh: "杠杆上限：", en: "Leverage cap: " })}
                  {L(product.leverageCap)}
                </li>
                <li>
                  {L({ zh: "赔付额上限：", en: "Payout cap: " })}
                  {L(product.payoutCap)}
                </li>
                <li>
                  {L({ zh: "等待期：", en: "Waiting period: " })}
                  {L(product.waitPeriod)}
                </li>
              </ul>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

用法（示例）：

已单独提取为“报价计算”组件。可直接放入 `src/components/QuoteCalculator.tsx`。

```tsx
// src/components/QuoteCalculator.tsx
import React, { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type Locale = "zh-CN" | "en-US";
type Quote = {
  payout: number;
  premium: number;
  premiumRatio: number;
  payoutRatio: number;
  quoteId: string;
  valid: string;
  validTs: number;
  pricingVersion: string;
};

const QUOTE_TTL_MIN = 10;
const LEVERAGE_MAX = 100;
const PRICING_VERSION = "pl-formula-v1";

function calcPremium(principal: number, leverage: number) {
  const baseRatio = 0.05 + (leverage - 20) * 0.001 + (principal / 500) * 0.02;
  const premiumRatio = Math.min(0.15, baseRatio);
  return { premium: +(premiumRatio * principal).toFixed(2), premiumRatio: +premiumRatio.toFixed(4) };
}
function calcPayoutRatio(principal: number, leverage: number) {
  const baseRatio = 0.25 + (leverage - 50) * 0.005 - (principal / 500) * 0.1;
  return Math.min(0.5, Math.max(0.1, baseRatio));
}
function calcPayout(principal: number, leverage: number) {
  const ratio = calcPayoutRatio(principal, leverage);
  return { payout: +(ratio * principal).toFixed(2), payoutRatio: +ratio.toFixed(4) };
}

const DICT = {
  zh: {
    title: "报价计算（仅演示）",
    principal: "本金 (USDT)",
    leverage: "杠杆 (×，≤100)",
    btn: "生成报价",
    ttl: "有效期（10 分钟）",
    quote_id: "报价编号",
    valid_until: "有效期（10 分钟）",
    version: "价格版本",
    err: "本金需在 50–500 USDT，杠杆需在 1–100。",
    premium: "保费",
    payout: "赔付额",
    premium_ratio: "保费比例",
    payout_ratio: "赔付比例",
    sub: "仅围绕 本金 / 杠杆 / 保费 / 赔付额；自动生成 proof.json 并随申赔提交。",
  },
  en: {
    title: "Quote (Demo)",
    principal: "Principal (USDT)",
    leverage: "Leverage (×, ≤100)",
    btn: "Get Quote",
    ttl: "Valid Until (10 min)",
    quote_id: "Quote ID",
    valid_until: "Valid Until (10 min)",
    version: "Pricing Version",
    err: "Principal 50–500 USDT, leverage 1–100.",
    premium: "Premium",
    payout: "Payout",
    premium_ratio: "Premium Ratio",
    payout_ratio: "Payout Ratio",
    sub: "Only principal / leverage / premium / payout. Auto-generate proof.json for claims.",
  },
};

function Stat({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white/5 border border-white/10">
      <div className="text-xs text-white/60">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-lg font-semibold mt-1">{value}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function QuoteCalculator({
  locale = "zh-CN",
  defaultPrincipal = 500,
  defaultLeverage = 10,
  onQuote,
}: {
  locale?: Locale;
  defaultPrincipal?: number;
  defaultLeverage?: number;
  onQuote?: (q: Quote) => void;
}) {
  const t = useMemo(() => (locale === "en-US" ? DICT.en : DICT.zh), [locale]);
  const [principal, setPrincipal] = useState<number | string>(defaultPrincipal);
  const [leverage, setLeverage] = useState<number | string>(defaultLeverage);
  const [quote, setQuote] = useState<Quote | null>(null);
  const [err, setErr] = useState("");

  const handleGetQuote = useCallback(() => {
    const P = Number(principal);
    const L = Number(leverage);
    if (!Number.isFinite(P) || !Number.isFinite(L) || P < 50 || P > 500 || L < 1 || L > LEVERAGE_MAX) {
      setQuote(null);
      setErr(t.err);
      return;
    }
    setErr("");
    const { premium, premiumRatio } = calcPremium(P, L);
    const { payout, payoutRatio } = calcPayout(P, L);
    const quoteId = `Q-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`;
    const validTs = Date.now() + QUOTE_TTL_MIN * 60 * 1000;
    const valid = new Date(validTs).toLocaleString();
    const q: Quote = { payout, premium, premiumRatio, payoutRatio, quoteId, valid, validTs, pricingVersion: PRICING_VERSION };
    setQuote(q);
    onQuote?.(q);
  }, [principal, leverage, t.err, onQuote]);

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1.2fr,0.8fr] text-slate-100">
      {/* 左侧表单卡片 */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-6 space-y-4">
        <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold">{t.title}</h3>

        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <label className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm text-white/70">
            <span>{t.principal}</span>
            <input
              className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-indigo-400 focus:outline-none"
              type="number"
              min={0}
              value={principal}
              onChange={(e) => setPrincipal(e.target.value)}
            />
          </label>

          <label className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm text-white/70">
            <span>{t.leverage}</span>
            <input
              className="rounded-xl border border-white/10 bg-black/30 px-3 py-2 text-white focus:border-indigo-400 focus:outline-none"
              type="number"
              min={1}
              max={LEVERAGE_MAX}
              value={leverage}
              onChange={(e) => setLeverage(e.target.value)}
            />
          </label>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleGetQuote}
            className="rounded-full bg-indigo-500 px-5 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-700/30 transition hover:bg-indigo-400"
          >
            {t.btn}
          </button>
          <span className="text-xs text-white/60">
            {t.ttl}：{QUOTE_TTL_MIN} {locale === "en-US" ? "minutes" : "分钟"}
          </span>
        </div>

        {err && <p className="text-sm text-rose-300">{err}</p>}

        {quote && (
          <div className="space-y-1 text-xs text-white/60">
            <p>{t.quote_id}：{quote.quoteId}</p>
            <p>{t.valid_until}：{quote.valid}</p>
            <p>{t.version}：{quote.pricingVersion}</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 右侧统计卡片 */}
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Stat label={t.premium} value={quote ? `${quote.premium.toFixed(2)} USDT` : "—"} />
          <Stat label={t.payout} value={quote ? `${quote.payout.toFixed(2)} USDT` : "—"} />
          <Stat label={t.premium_ratio} value={quote ? `${(quote.premiumRatio * 100).toFixed(2)}%` : "—"} />
          <Stat label={t.payout_ratio} value={quote ? `${(quote.payoutRatio * 100).toFixed(2)}%` : "—"} />
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-3xl border border-white/10 bg-white/5 p-5 text-sm text-white/70 leading-relaxed">
          {t.sub}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

用法示例（页面中放置该区域即可渲染与截图一致的模块）：

  return (

      </div>
    </main>

}

<!-- mid: b96da459-1df3-4109-9f3c-7dffd5734357 -->
<details>
<summary>🧠 思考过程 (Thinking Process - 点击展开)</summary>

> **[解读用户请求]**
> 用户需要处理 USDC 购买订单的逻辑，具体在 Base 链上。他们可能需要接入 ERC-20 批准、transferFrom，或者用到支付处理器或 Permit2 和 EIP-2612 许可。他们要求现有的开源库，例如 Uniswap SDK、0x 或 Coinbase Onramp。
>
> **[避免具体地址]**
> 避免直接使用具体地址，最好通过 ERC-20 地址作为参数。对于支付和收据逻辑，可以参考以下成熟方案：  
> - OpenZeppelin 合约：ERC-20 接口、SafeERC20、Ownable、ReentrancyGuard；使用 SafeERC20.safeTransferFrom。  
> - Uniswap 和 Permit2 用于更高效的跨链批准。  
> - USDC 支持 EIP-3009，尚需验证。
>
> **[提供简洁代码方案]**

<!-- mid: 6275129e-c4fb-4e26-9c87-90029e01dce9 -->
结论：用 OpenZeppelin + Uniswap Permit2 最稳。  
合约用 SafeERC20 接受 USDC；优先走 Permit2 一签到位，fallback 走 approve+transferFrom。前端用 viem/wagmi 或 ethers。

# 合约（可直接用）
```solidity
// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
pragma solidity ^0.8.20;

import {IERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
import {SafeERC20} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";
import {Ownable} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/access/Ownable.sol";
import {ReentrancyGuard} from "@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/ReentrancyGuard.sol";

/* ===== Uniswap Permit2 最小接口 ===== */
interface ISignatureTransfer {
  struct TokenPermissions { address token; uint256 amount; } // 授权的 token 和最大金额
  struct PermitTransferFrom {
    TokenPermissions permitted;
    uint256 nonce;
    uint256 deadline;
  }
  struct SignatureTransferDetails { address to; uint256 requestedAmount; }
  function permitTransferFrom(
    PermitTransferFrom calldata permit,
    SignatureTransferDetails calldata transferDetails,
    address owner,
    bytes calldata signature
  ) external;
}

contract LiqPassCheckout is Ownable, ReentrancyGuard {
  using SafeERC20 for IERC20;

  event OrderPaid(bytes32 indexed orderId, address indexed payer, uint256 amount, bool viaPermit2);
  error OrderUsed();
  error InvalidAmount();

  address public immutable USDC;          // Base 上的 USDC 地址（部署后设置）
  address public immutable TREASURY;      // 收款地址
  ISignatureTransfer public immutable PERMIT2; // Permit2 合约地址（多链固定：0x000000000022D473030F116dDEE9F6B43aC78BA3）

  mapping(bytes32 => bool) public consumed;

  constructor(address usdc, address treasury, address permit2) {
    USDC = usdc;
    TREASURY = treasury;
    PERMIT2 = ISignatureTransfer(permit2);
  }

  // 方案A：approve + transferFrom
  function buyWithUSDC(bytes32 orderId, uint256 amount) external nonReentrant {
    if (consumed[orderId]) revert OrderUsed();
    if (amount == 0) revert InvalidAmount();
    consumed[orderId] = true;
    IERC20(USDC).safeTransferFrom(msg.sender, TREASURY, amount);
    emit OrderPaid(orderId, msg.sender, amount, false);
  }

  // 方案B：Permit2 一次签名直付
  function buyWithPermit2(
    bytes32 orderId,
    ISignatureTransfer.PermitTransferFrom calldata permit,
    bytes calldata signature,
    uint256 amount   // 实际请求金额
  ) external nonReentrant {
    if (consumed[orderId]) revert OrderUsed();
    if (amount == 0) revert InvalidAmount();
    // 安全性：限制 token 与金额上限
    require(permit.permitted.token == USDC, "token!=USDC");
    require(amount <= permit.permitted.amount, "amount>permit");
    consumed[orderId] = true;

    ISignatureTransfer.SignatureTransferDetails memory details =
      ISignatureTransfer.SignatureTransferDetails({ to: TREASURY, requestedAmount: amount });

    PERMIT2.permitTransferFrom(permit, details, msg.sender, signature);
    emit OrderPaid(orderId, msg.sender, amount, true);
  }
}
```

> 部署参数：`usdc`=Base USDC 地址，`treasury`=你的收款地址，`permit2`=`0x000000000022D473030F116dDEE9F6B43aC78BA3`。  
> 订单 id 用后不可复用（防重放）。

# 前端（viem 示例）
```ts
import { createWalletClient, custom, parseUnits } from "viem";
import { base } from "viem/chains";
import { SignatureTransfer, PERMIT2_ADDRESS, MaxUint256 } from "@uniswap/permit2-sdk";
import abi from "./LiqPassCheckout.json"; // 上面合约ABI

const USDC_DEC = 6;

async function buyWithApprove(checkoutAddr:string, usdcAddr:string, orderId:string, usdcAmount:string){
  const client = createWalletClient({ chain: base, transport: custom(window.ethereum) });
  const amount = parseUnits(usdcAmount, USDC_DEC);                    // "10" -> 10 USDC
  // 1) 先授权
  await client.writeContract({ address: usdcAddr, abi: [
    { "name":"approve","type":"function","stateMutability":"nonpayable",
      "inputs":[{"name":"spender","type":"address"},{"name":"amount","type":"uint256"}],
      "outputs":[{"type":"bool"}] } as const
  ], functionName:"approve", args:[checkoutAddr, amount]});
  // 2) 下单
  await client.writeContract({
    address: checkoutAddr, abi, functionName:"buyWithUSDC",
    args:[`0x${orderId}`, amount]
  });
}

async function buyWithPermit2(checkoutAddr:string, usdcAddr:string, orderId:string, usdcAmount:string){
  const client = createWalletClient({ chain: base, transport: custom(window.ethereum) });
  const [account] = await client.getAddresses();
  const amount = parseUnits(usdcAmount, USDC_DEC);

  // 构造 EIP-712
  const now = Math.floor(Date.now()/1000);
  const permit = {
    permitted: { token: usdcAddr, amount: MaxUint256 }, // 上限给足，实际支付用 requestedAmount 限定
    nonce: crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0],
    deadline: now + 3600
  };

  const { domain, types, values } = SignatureTransfer.getPermitData(permit, PERMIT2_ADDRESS, base.id);
  const signature = await client.signTypedData({ account, domain, types, primaryType:"PermitTransferFrom", message: values });

  // 调用合约
  await client.writeContract({
    address: checkoutAddr, abi, functionName:"buyWithPermit2",
    args:[
      `0x${orderId}`,
      permit,
      signature as `0x${string}`,
      amount
    ]
  });
}
```

# 选型要点
- **合约库**：OpenZeppelin Contracts（IERC20、SafeERC20、ReentrancyGuard、Ownable）。  
- **签名支付**：Uniswap **Permit2**（统一支持包括 USDC 在内的大多数 ERC-20；USDC 不依赖 EIP-2612）。  
- **前端**：viem/wagmi 或 ethers。金额用 6 位小数处理。  
- **风控**：订单去重、金额上限校验、收款金库只读、事件日志。

<!-- mid: dc32a5bd-8045-417d-8be2-71629059faec -->
用 **Uniswap Permit2 + OpenZeppelin SafeERC20**。

- 结论：USDC 不支持 EIP-2612 原生 permit，行业成熟做法是 **Permit2 一次签名直付**，并保留 **approve+transferFrom 回退**。  
- 合约库：`@openzeppelin/contracts`（`SafeERC20`、`ReentrancyGuard`）+ **Permit2 接口**。  
- 前端库：`@uniswap/permit2-sdk` + `viem/wagmi` 或 `ethers`。  
- 地址：
  - **Permit2**：`0x000000000022D473030F116dDEE9F6B43aC78BA3`（多链统一）  
  - **USDC(Base 主网)**：`0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913`，decimals=6  
  - **USDC(Base Sepolia)**：`0x036CbD53842c5426634e7929541eC2318f3dCF7e`，decimals=6

实现要点（最少集）：
- 合约暴露两个入口：`buyWithPermit2(orderId, permit, sig, amount)` 与 `buyWithUSDC(orderId, amount)`；用 `mapping(bytes32=>bool)` 做订单去重。
- 前端优先走 Permit2（`SignatureTransfer.getPermitData` 组 EIP-712），失败再走 `approve + buyWithUSDC`。
- 金额统一用 6 位小数处理，链上校验 `token==USDC && amount<=permit.permitted.amount`。
